# OpenAlex author ids for the researcher roster

Generated 2026-10-13 14:21 UTC by `tools/resolve_openalex_authors.py` (round 5 (RUN) B7b, C4 of `docs/design/collection-yield.md`; coauthor evidence, T-ARX). Open: the ambiguous and not-found names below are undecided; the resolved ids are already in the roster.

- Roster: `configs/rosters/researchers.yaml` version 1.2.0 → 1.3.1; 2239 names considered.
- Name rule: the round-5 B7b cache (live run `resolve-oa-authors-1791874758`, $0.6891). Coauthor evidence, live 2026-10-13 (keyed, OpenAlex ledger): `resolve-oa-authors-1791897217` (works 280 pages, details 37; $0.0317), `resolve-oa-authors-1791897746` (round 2: works 87, details 10; $0.0097), `resolve-oa-authors-1791898437` (works 5, details 1; $0.0006), `resolve-oa-authors-1791900892` (with the guard: works 5, details 1; $0.0006); probe and check fetches $0.0004. This report: decided offline from the caches with `--rounds 2` over roster 1.2.0 (the 1.3.0 ids are recomputed, not read).
- Precision check of the coauthor rule (30 random new decisions: 16 `coauthors`, 10 `split`, 4 `single_inactive`; up to 5 recent titles of each accepted profile read against the name): under a first, looser relevance test (AI share ≥ 20% sufficed) 2 of 30 wrong (Fang Liu → a merged profile shown as "Zhiyuan Liu", 686 works since 2019; Jiashu Xu → a medical-imaging namesake holding 6 of his works), hence the shared-fraction test; on a fresh sample under the final rule 1 of 30 wrong (Michael Petrov: astrophysics and RS-232 works beside the Distill/OpenAI ones, AI share 0%; counted wrong, not settled), and 4 right-person profiles carry a few namesake works (Jeff Wu, Yun He, the added Eric Wallace and Satyapriya Krishna splits).
- Not adopted: a split extension (profiles with 20-33% shared works joining when they share ≥ 3 roster coauthors with the accepted ones; it would have added Jan Kautz's and Alina Oprea's main profiles). Of 30 of its 54 added ids read, 4 were namesake profiles (Jinsung Yoon `A5002289527`, Chang Zhou `A5102025315`, Jingren Zhou `A5057864403`, Hongkun Yu `A5101433901`) and 5 mixed (He He, Zifan Wang, Jaehun Jung, Yike Wang, Yanfei Chen); those main profiles stay in "Possible split profiles".
- Rule (names): candidate = name match (`names_match`); active = ≥ 2 works and a work in the last 3 years; one active candidate → resolved if adjacent-field share ≥ 30% or a configured institution; several → the only one at a configured institution (CS share ≥ 20%; for a common name a last known one), else, for a name that is not common, the top one when it has ≥ 5 CS works, CS share ≥ 20% and 3x the next one's CS works. Common = on the roster's `disambiguate` list or ≥ 5 active candidates. A profile named with a first initial only is not a candidate. Details in the tool's docstring.
- Rule (coauthors): the works since 2019-01-01 of every resolved roster id, those with at most 99 authorships; their name-matching authors join the candidates. Coauthor link = ≥ 2 distinct resolved roster coauthors over ≥ 2 distinct works (the name itself and its roster namesakes do not count; roster spellings sharing an id are one person; a profile that is another roster person's id is theirs). A profile counts only when ≥ 33% of its works since 2019-01-01 are shared with roster people (else a merged namesake: it neither resolves nor blocks). An ambiguous or not-found name resolves (`coauthors`) when its coauthor-linked profiles are one person: the top one (most roster coauthors; for a common name ≥ 3) and its splits, each linked and sharing ≥ 1 roster coauthor with it (common name: ≥ 2), or carrying its ORCID and sharing one; a linked profile left over, or one with a different ORCID, keeps the name ambiguous. A name with exactly one name-matching profile, not active, with CS share ≥ 20% or a configured institution, resolves (`single_inactive`). Resolved names gain splits by the same split rule; no id is removed.
- Coauthor round 1: 1499 resolved ids indexed (0 without fetched works), 37241 works used, 250 fetched works skipped (> 99 authorships), 1446 candidates from works; 267 names newly resolved, 168 split ids added.
- Coauthor round 2: 1919 resolved ids indexed (0 without fetched works), 39142 works used, 250 fetched works skipped (> 99 authorships), 1520 candidates from works; 286 names newly resolved, 201 split ids added.
- Candidate cells: `id` name; works; CS share and AI share (subfields 1702, 1707) of topic counts; works in the last 3 years; last known institution; configured org; ORCID; resolved roster coauthors (most shared works first) and the distinct works shared.
